## Narrative Analysis

The strategy uses 4h and 1d trend alignment to gate directional bias, then executes with 15m/1h entries. News impact windows modulate exposure rather than acting as standalone triggers. Reliability caution: negative_sharpe, profit_factor_below_one, negative_total_return.
